198. saveLocalfunção · linhas 12944–12960 · usa 60, 122, 131, 133, 137, 147, 148, 157, 181, 183, 187, 197, 207, 233, 241, 245, 255, 256, 263, 292
    function saveLocal() {
      const record = getProjectRecord();
      const idx = state.localProjects.findIndex((p) => p.id === state.currentId);

      if (idx >= 0) {
        state.localProjects[idx] = record;
      } else {
        state.currentId = record.id;
        state.localProjects.unshift(record);
      }

      localStorage.setItem(LOCAL_PROJECTS_KEY, JSON.stringify(state.localProjects));
      persistState();
      renderProjects();
      showStatus("success", "Projeto salvo localmente.");
    }
199. loadProjectfunção · linhas 12961–12975 · usa 122, 131, 133, 137, 146, 149, 161, 163, 181, 183, 187, 196, 211, 213, 233, 241, 245, 254, 259, 267, 269, 292
    function loadProject(project) {
      state.currentId = project.id;
      state.title = project.title || "Sem título";
      state.html = project.html || "";
      state.css = project.css || "";
      state.js = project.js || "";
      state.tab = "html";

      persistState();
      renderAll();
      updatePreview();
      closeOpenModal();
      showStatus("success", "Projeto aberto: " + state.title);
    }
200. deleteProjectfunção · linhas 12976–13000 · usa 60, 63, 122, 131, 133, 150, 155, 157, 181, 183, 205, 207, 233, 241, 258, 260, 263, 292
    async function deleteProject(project) {
      if (!confirm('Excluir "' + project.title + '"?')) return;

      if (project.source === "cloud" && state.supabase) {
        const { error } = await state.supabase
          .from("snippets")
          .delete()
          .eq("id", project.id);

        if (error) {
          showStatus("error", "Erro ao excluir: " + error.message);
          return;
        }

        await loadCloudProjects();
        showStatus("success", "Projeto excluído do Supabase.");
        return;
      }

      state.localProjects = state.localProjects.filter((p) => p.id !== project.id);
      localStorage.setItem(LOCAL_PROJECTS_KEY, JSON.stringify(state.localProjects));
      renderProjects();
      showStatus("success", "Projeto local excluído.");
    }
201. renameProjectfunção · linhas 13001–13023 · usa 60, 131, 151, 155, 157, 181, 205, 207, 233, 258, 261, 263
    function renameProject(project) {
      const title = prompt("Novo nome:", project.title);
      if (!title || !title.trim()) return;

      if (project.source === "cloud" && state.supabase) {
        state.supabase
          .from("snippets")
          .update({ title: title.trim() })
          .eq("id", project.id)
          .then(() => loadCloudProjects());
        return;
      }

      const local = state.localProjects.find((p) => p.id === project.id);
      if (local) {
        local.title = title.trim();
        localStorage.setItem(LOCAL_PROJECTS_KEY, JSON.stringify(state.localProjects));
        renderProjects();
      }
    }

    // ===== SUPABASE =====
202. connectSupabasefunção · linhas 13024–13043 · usa 118, 131, 132, 134, 152, 181, 182, 184, 233, 234, 242, 283
    function connectSupabase() {
      const url = $("supabaseUrl").value.trim();
      const key = $("supabaseKey").value.trim();

      if (!url || !key) {
        showConfigStatus("Informe URL e chave.", "error");
        return false;
      }

      if (!window.supabase) {
        showConfigStatus("Biblioteca Supabase não carregada.", "error");
        return false;
      }

      state.supabase = window.supabase.createClient(url, key);
      localStorage.setItem(CONFIG_KEY, JSON.stringify({ url, key }));

      return true;
    }
203. testSupabasefunção · linhas 13044–13064 · usa 122, 131, 133, 134, 152, 153, 155, 181, 183, 184, 202, 205, 233, 241, 242, 258, 292
    async function testSupabase() {
      if (!connectSupabase()) return;

      showConfigStatus("Testando tabela snippets...");

      const { error } = await state.supabase
        .from("snippets")
        .select("id")
        .limit(1);

      if (error) {
        showConfigStatus("Erro: " + error.message, "error");
        showStatus("error", "Falha na conexão Supabase.");
        return;
      }

      showConfigStatus("Supabase conectado com sucesso!", "success");
      showStatus("success", "Conexão confirmada.");
      await loadCloudProjects();
    }
204. saveCloudfunção · linhas 13065–13105 · usa 122, 131, 133, 137, 152, 154, 155, 181, 183, 187, 202, 205, 233, 241, 245, 257, 258, 292
    async function saveCloud() {
      if (!state.supabase && !connectSupabase()) {
        showStatus("error", "Configure Supabase antes de salvar.");
        return;
      }

      const payload = {
        title: state.title.trim() || "Sem título",
        html: state.html,
        css: state.css,
        js: state.js
      };

      let result;

      if (state.currentId && state.currentId.length > 20) {
        result = await state.supabase
          .from("snippets")
          .update(payload)
          .eq("id", state.currentId)
          .select("id")
          .single();
      } else {
        result = await state.supabase
          .from("snippets")
          .insert(payload)
          .select("id")
          .single();
      }

      if (result.error) {
        showStatus("error", "Erro ao salvar: " + result.error.message);
        return;
      }

      state.currentId = result.data.id;
      persistState();
      await loadCloudProjects();
      showStatus("success", "Projeto salvo no Supabase.");
    }
205. loadCloudProjectsfunção · linhas 13106–13128 · usa 60, 122, 131, 133, 155, 157, 181, 183, 207, 233, 241, 258, 263, 292
    async function loadCloudProjects() {
      if (!state.supabase) return;

      const { data, error } = await state.supabase
        .from("snippets")
        .select("id,title,html,css,js")
        .order("title", { ascending: true });

      if (error) {
        showStatus("error", "Erro ao carregar: " + error.message);
        return;
      }

      state.cloudProjects = (data || []).map((p) => ({
        ...p,
        source: "cloud"
      }));

      renderProjects();
    }

    // ===== RENDERIZAÇÃO =====
206. mergedProjectsfunção · linhas 13129–13134 · usa 131, 156, 181, 233, 262
    function mergedProjects() {
      const cloud = state.cloudProjects.map((p) => ({ ...p, source: "cloud" }));
      const local = state.localProjects.map((p) => ({ ...p, source: "local" }));
      return [...cloud, ...local];
    }
207. renderProjectsfunção · linhas 13135–13154 · usa 60, 118, 132, 156, 157, 158, 159, 182, 206, 208, 209, 234, 262, 263, 264, 265, 283
    function renderProjects() {
      const term = $("searchInput").value.trim().toLowerCase();
      const projects = mergedProjects().filter((p) =>
        String(p.title || "").toLowerCase().includes(term)
      );

      $("projectList").innerHTML = "";

      if (!projects.length) {
        $("projectList").innerHTML = '<div class="empty">Nenhum projeto.</div>';
        return;
      }

      projects.forEach((p, idx) => {
        $("projectList").appendChild(createProjectElement(p, idx + 1));
      });

      renderOpenProjects();
    }
208. createProjectElementfunção · linhas 13155–13197 · usa 63, 149, 150, 151, 158, 199, 200, 201, 259, 260, 261, 264
    function createProjectElement(project, number) {
      const item = document.createElement("div");
      item.className = "project-item";

      const info = document.createElement("div");
      info.className = "project-info";

      const name = document.createElement("div");
      name.className = "project-name";
      name.textContent = "#" + number + " — " + (project.title || "Sem título");

      const meta = document.createElement("div");
      meta.className = "project-meta";
      meta.textContent = (project.source === "cloud" ? "☁ Supabase" : "💾 Local");

      info.append(name, meta);
      info.addEventListener("click", () => loadProject(project));

      const actions = document.createElement("div");
      actions.className = "project-actions";

      const rename = document.createElement("button");
      rename.className = "small-btn";
      rename.textContent = "✎";
      rename.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        renameProject(project);
      });

      const del = document.createElement("button");
      del.className = "small-btn";
      del.textContent = "×";
      del.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        deleteProject(project);
      });

      actions.append(rename, del);
      item.append(info, actions);

      return item;
    }
209. renderOpenProjectsfunção · linhas 13198–13216 · usa 118, 132, 156, 158, 159, 182, 206, 208, 234, 262, 264, 265, 283
    function renderOpenProjects() {
      const term = $("openSearchInput").value.trim().toLowerCase();
      const container = $("openProjectList");
      container.innerHTML = "";

      const projects = mergedProjects().filter((p) =>
        String(p.title || "").toLowerCase().includes(term)
      );

      if (!projects.length) {
        container.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">Nenhum projeto.</div>';
        return;
      }

      projects.forEach((p, idx) => {
        container.appendChild(createProjectElement(p, idx + 1));
      });
    }

    function renderLayout() {
      $("editorPanel").classList.toggle("collapsed", state.editorCollapsed);
      $("expandEditorBtn").style.display = state.editorCollapsed ? "inline-flex" : "none";
      $("collapseBtn").style.display = state.editorCollapsed ? "none" : "inline-flex";

      const isMobile = window.innerWidth <= 600;

        $("editorPanel").classList.toggle("mobile-hidden", state.mobileView !== "editor");
        $("previewPanel").classList.toggle("mobile-hidden", state.mobileView !== "preview");
      } else {
        $("editorPanel").classList.remove("mobile-hidden");
        $("previewPanel").classList.remove("mobile-hidden");
      }
    }

214. downloadFilefunção · linhas 13256–13269 · usa 164, 270
    function downloadFile(filename, content, type) {
      const blob = new Blob([content], { type });
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const link = document.createElement("a");

      link.href = url;
      link.download = filename;
      document.body.appendChild(link);
      link.click();
      link.remove();

      setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000);
    }

    function openFullscreen() {
      const blob = new Blob([buildDocument()], { type: "text/html;charset=utf-8" });
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const win = window.open(url, "_blank", "noopener,noreferrer");
      
      if (!win) {
        showStatus("error", "Abra a janela popup manualmente.");
      }

      setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 5000);
    }
218. importFilefunção · linhas 13300–13329 · usa 118, 122, 131, 132, 133, 137, 146, 161, 167, 181, 182, 183, 187, 196, 211, 233, 234, 241, 245, 254, 267, 274, 283, 292
    function importFile(file) {
      const reader = new FileReader();

      reader.onload = () => {
        const content = String(reader.result || "");
        const ext = file.name.split(".").pop().toLowerCase();

        state.title = file.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, "") || "Importado";

        if (ext === "css") {
          state.tab = "css";
          state.css = content;
        } else if (ext === "js") {
          state.tab = "js";
          state.js = content;
        } else {
          state.tab = "html";
          state.html = content;
        }

        state.currentId = null;
        persistState();
        renderAll();
        updatePreview();
        showStatus("success", "Arquivo importado.");
      };

      reader.readAsText(file);
    }
219. resetCurrentfunção · linhas 13330–13343 · usa 122, 128, 129, 130, 131, 133, 137, 146, 161, 168, 178, 179, 180, 181, 183, 187, 196, 211, 229, 230, 231, 233, 241, 245, 254, 267, 275, 292

      state.tab = "html";

    }

221. parseCsvfunção · linhas 13357–13405 · usa 170
    function parseCsv(text) {
      const rows = [];
      let row = [];
      let value = "";
      let quoted = false;

      text = text.replace(/^\uFEFF/, "");

      for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {
        const char = text[i];
        const next = text[i + 1];

        if (char === '"' && quoted && next === '"') {
          value += '"';
          i++;
        } else if (char === '"') {
          quoted = !quoted;
        } else if (char === "," && !quoted) {
          row.push(value);
          value = "";
        } else if ((char === "\n" || char === "\r") && !quoted) {
          if (char === "\r" && next === "\n") i++;
          row.push(value);
          if (row.some((item) => item.trim())) rows.push(row);
          row = [];
          value = "";
        } else {
          value += char;
        }
      }

      if (value || row.length) {
        row.push(value);
        rows.push(row);
      }

      if (rows.length < 2) return [];

      const headers = rows[0].map((h) => h.trim().toLowerCase());

      return rows.slice(1).map((cols) => {
        const result = {};
        headers.forEach((h, i) => {
          result[h] = cols[i] || "";
        });
        return result;
      });
    }
222. importCsvFilefunção · linhas 13406–13437 · usa 60, 122, 131, 133, 157, 170, 171, 181, 183, 207, 221, 233, 241, 263, 292
    async function importCsvFile(file) {
      const text = await file.text();
      const rows = parseCsv(text);

      if (!rows.length) {
        showStatus("error", "CSV vazio ou inválido.");
        return;
      }

      let imported = 0;

      for (const row of rows) {
        const project = {
          id: crypto.randomUUID(),
          title: row.title || "Importado",
          html: row.html || "",
          css: row.css || "",
          js: row.js || "",
          saved_at: new Date().toISOString()
        };

        state.localProjects.unshift(project);
        imported++;
      }

      localStorage.setItem(LOCAL_PROJECTS_KEY, JSON.stringify(state.localProjects));
      renderProjects();
      showStatus("success", imported + " projeto(s) importado(s).");
    }

225. bindEventsfunção · linhas 13452–13567 · usa 60, 118, 122, 131, 132, 133, 134, 137, 139, 141, 146, 148, 153, 154, 155, 157, 159, 160, 162, 163, 165, 166, 167, 168, 169, 171, 172, 174, 181, 182, 183, 184, 187, 189, 191, 196, 198, 203, 204, 205, 207, 209, 210, 212, 213, 215, 216, 217, 218, 219, 220, 222, 223, 233, 234, 241, 242, 245, 247, 249, 254, 256, 257, 258, 263, 265, 266, 268, 269, 271, 272, 273, 274, 275, 276, 277, 279, 283, 292
    function bindEvents() {
      // Abas
      document.querySelectorAll("[data-tab]").forEach((btn) => {
        btn.addEventListener("click", () => setTab(btn.dataset.tab));
      });

      // Título do projeto
      $("projectTitle").addEventListener("input", (e) => {
        state.title = e.target.value;
        persistState();
      });

      // Editor
      $("codeEditor").addEventListener("input", (e) => {
        setCurrentCode(e.target.value);
      });

      // Botões principais
      $("autoRunBtn").addEventListener("click", () => {
        state.autoRun = !state.autoRun;
        persistState();
        renderLayout();
        if (state.autoRun) updatePreview();
      });

      $("runBtn").addEventListener("click", updatePreview);
      $("saveLocalBtn").addEventListener("click", saveLocal);
      $("saveCloudBtn").addEventListener("click", saveCloud);
      $("openBtn").addEventListener("click", openOpenModal);
      $("closeOpenBtn").addEventListener("click", closeOpenModal);

      $("copyBtn").addEventListener("click", copyCurrent);
      $("downloadBtn").addEventListener("click", downloadCurrent);
      $("fullscreenBtn").addEventListener("click", openFullscreen);
      $("resetBtn").addEventListener("click", resetCurrent);
      $("clearBtn").addEventListener("click", clearCurrent);
      $("themeBtn").addEventListener("click", toggleTheme);

      // Collapse editor
      $("collapseBtn").addEventListener("click", () => {
        state.editorCollapsed = true;
        persistState();
        renderLayout();
      });

      $("expandEditorBtn").addEventListener("click", () => {
        state.editorCollapsed = false;
        persistState();
        renderLayout();
      });

      // Mobile
      $("mobileEditorBtn").addEventListener("click", () => {
        state.mobileView = "editor";
        renderLayout();
      });

      $("mobilePreviewBtn").addEventListener("click", () => {
        state.mobileView = "preview";
        updatePreview();
        renderLayout();
      });

      // Configuração
      $("configBtn").addEventListener("click", () => {
        $("configModal").classList.add("open");
      });

      $("closeConfigBtn").addEventListener("click", () => {
        $("configModal").classList.remove("open");
      });

      $("testSupabaseBtn").addEventListener("click", testSupabase);

      $("clearSupabaseBtn").addEventListener("click", () => {
        $("supabaseUrl").value = "";
        $("supabaseKey").value = "";
        state.supabase = null;
        localStorage.removeItem(CONFIG_KEY);
        showConfigStatus("Configuração removida.");
      });

      // Importar arquivo
      $("importBtn").addEventListener("click", () => $("fileInput").click());

      $("fileInput").addEventListener("change", (e) => {
        const file = e.target.files[0];
        if (file) importFile(file);
        e.target.value = "";
      });

      // Importar CSV
      $("importCsvBtn").addEventListener("click", () => $("csvInput").click());

      $("csvInput").addEventListener("change", async (e) => {
        const file = e.target.files[0];
        if (file) await importCsvFile(file);
        e.target.value = "";
      });

      // Pesquisa
      $("searchInput").addEventListener("input", renderProjects);
      $("openSearchInput").addEventListener("input", renderOpenProjects);

      // Atualizar
      $("refreshBtn").addEventListener("click", () => {
        loadCloudProjects();
        showStatus("info", "Projetos atualizados.");
      });

      // Resize
      window.addEventListener("resize", renderLayout);
    }

    // ===== INICIALIZAÇÃO =====
226. startupfunção · linhas 13568–14425 · usa 56, 131, 135, 136, 146, 155, 173, 174, 175, 181, 185, 186, 196, 205, 224, 225, 233, 243, 244, 254, 258, 278, 279
    async function startup() {
      applyTheme();
      bindEvents();
      loadState();
      updatePreview();

      // Carregar config Supabase se existir
      const config = safeJsonParse(localStorage.getItem(CONFIG_KEY), {});
      if (config.url && config.key && window.supabase) {
        state.supabase = window.supabase.createClient(config.url, config.key);
        await loadCloudProjects();
      }
    }

    // Iniciar quando DOM estiver pronto
    if (document.readyState === "loading") {
      document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", startup);
    } else {
      startup();
    }
  </script>
</body>
</html>

235. initSupabasefunção · linhas 14493–14505 · usa 119, 238, 284
    async function initSupabase() {
      const url = localStorage.getItem("supabase_url");
      const key = localStorage.getItem("supabase_key");

      if (!url || !key) {
        showLoginStatus("Configure Supabase primeiro.", "error");
        return false;
      }

      supabase = window.supabase.createClient(url, key);
      return true;
    }
236. handleLoginfunção · linhas 14506–14531 · usa 118, 119, 132, 182, 234, 235, 238, 239, 283, 284
    async function handleLogin() {
      const email = $("loginEmail").value.trim();
      const password = $("loginPassword").value.trim();

      if (!email || !password) {
        showLoginStatus("Preencha email e senha.", "error");
        return;
      }

      if (!await initSupabase()) return;

      const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({
        email,
        password
      });

      if (error) {
        showLoginStatus("Erro: " + error.message, "error");
        return;
      }

      currentUser = data.user;
      showLoginStatus("Bem-vindo!", "success");
      setTimeout(showApp, 1000);
    }

        showLoginStatus("Preencha email e senha.", "error");
        return;

      if (!await initSupabase()) return;

        email,
        password
      });

      if (error) {
        showLoginStatus("Erro: " + error.message, "error");
        return;
      }

      showLoginStatus("Cadastro realizado! Verifique seu email.", "success");
    }

    }

    function safeJsonParse(value, fallback) {
      try {
        return JSON.parse(value);
      } catch {
        return fallback;
      }
    }

244. loadStatefunção · linhas 14615–14628 · usa 131, 135, 136, 161, 181, 185, 186, 211, 233, 243, 267
    function loadState() {
      const saved = safeJsonParse(localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY), null);
      if (saved) {
        Object.assign(state, saved);
      }

      state.localProjects = safeJsonParse(
        localStorage.getItem(LOCAL_PROJECTS_KEY),
        []
      );

      renderAll();
    }
245. persistStatefunção · linhas 14629–14647 · usa 131, 137, 181, 187, 233
    function persistState() {
      const copy = {
        mode: state.mode,
        tab: state.tab,
        title: state.title,
        html: state.html,
        css: state.css,
        js: state.js,
        currentId: state.currentId,
        autoRun: state.autoRun,
        editorCollapsed: state.editorCollapsed,
        mobileView: state.mobileView
      };

      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(copy));
    }

248. updateEditorfunção · linhas 14661–14673 · usa 118, 131, 132, 138, 140, 181, 182, 188, 190, 233, 234, 246, 283
    function updateEditor() {
      const value = getCurrentCode();
      $("codeEditor").value = value;

      const lines = Math.max(1, value.split("\n").length);
      $("lineNumbers").textContent = Array.from(
        { length: lines },
        (_, i) => i + 1
      ).join("\n");

      $("projectTitle").value = state.title;
    }

251. buildDocumentfunção · linhas 14689–14717 · usa 118, 131, 132, 143, 181, 182, 193, 233, 234, 283
    function buildDocument() {
      if (state.html.toLowerCase().includes("<!doctype")) {
        return state.html;
      }

      return `<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="pt-BR">
<head>
  <meta charset="UTF-8">
  <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
  <style>${state.css}</style>
</head>
<body>
  ${state.html}
  <script>
try {
${state.js}
} catch (error) {
  document.body.insertAdjacentHTML("beforeend",
    "<pre style='color:red;padding:12px;white-space:pre-wrap'>" +
    error.stack +
    "</pre>"
  );
}
  <\/script>
</body>
</html>`;
    }

254. updatePreviewfunção · linhas 14725–14741 · usa 118, 132, 137, 143, 146, 182, 187, 193, 196, 234, 245, 251, 283
    function updatePreview() {
      persistState();

      const blob = new Blob([buildDocument()], { type: "text/html;charset=utf-8" });
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const oldUrl = $("previewFrame").dataset.objectUrl;

      $("previewFrame").src = url;
      $("previewFrame").dataset.objectUrl = url;

      if (oldUrl) {
        setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(oldUrl), 1000);
      }
    }

255. getProjectRecordfunção · linhas 14742–14752 · usa 131, 147, 181, 197, 233
    function getProjectRecord() {
      return {
        id: state.currentId || crypto.randomUUID(),
        title: state.title.trim() || "Sem título",
        html: state.html,
        css: state.css,
        js: state.js,
        saved_at: new Date().toISOString()
      };
    }
256. saveLocalfunção · linhas 14753–14769 · usa 60, 122, 131, 133, 137, 147, 148, 157, 181, 183, 187, 197, 198, 207, 233, 241, 245, 255, 263, 292
    function saveLocal() {
      const record = getProjectRecord();
      const idx = state.localProjects.findIndex((p) => p.id === state.currentId);

      if (idx >= 0) {
        state.localProjects[idx] = record;
      } else {
        state.currentId = record.id;
        state.localProjects.unshift(record);
      }

      localStorage.setItem(LOCAL_PROJECTS_KEY, JSON.stringify(state.localProjects));
      persistState();
      renderProjects();
      showStatus("success", "Projeto salvo localmente.");
    }
257. saveCloudfunção · linhas 14770–14811 · usa 122, 131, 133, 137, 154, 155, 181, 183, 187, 204, 205, 233, 241, 245, 258, 292
    async function saveCloud() {
      if (!supabase || !currentUser) {
        showStatus("error", "Faça login primeiro.");
        return;
      }

      const payload = {
        user_id: currentUser.id,
        title: state.title.trim() || "Sem título",
        html: state.html,
        css: state.css,
        js: state.js
      };

      let result;

      if (state.currentId && state.currentId.length > 20) {
        result = await supabase
          .from("snippets")
          .update(payload)
          .eq("id", state.currentId)
          .select("id")
          .single();
      } else {
        result = await supabase
          .from("snippets")
          .insert(payload)
          .select("id")
          .single();
      }

      if (result.error) {
        showStatus("error", "Erro ao salvar: " + result.error.message);
        return;
      }

      state.currentId = result.data.id;
      persistState();
      await loadCloudProjects();
      showStatus("success", "Projeto salvo no Supabase!");
    }
258. loadCloudProjectsfunção · linhas 14812–14833 · usa 60, 131, 155, 157, 181, 205, 207, 233, 263
    async function loadCloudProjects() {
      if (!supabase || !currentUser) return;

      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from("snippets")
        .select("id,title,html,css,js")
        .eq("user_id", currentUser.id)
        .order("created_at", { ascending: false });

      if (error) {
        console.error("Erro ao carregar projetos:", error);
        return;
      }

      state.cloudProjects = (data || []).map((p) => ({
        ...p,
        source: "cloud"
      }));

      renderProjects();
    }
259. loadProjectfunção · linhas 14834–14848 · usa 122, 131, 133, 137, 146, 149, 161, 163, 181, 183, 187, 196, 199, 211, 213, 233, 241, 245, 254, 267, 269, 292
    function loadProject(project) {
      state.currentId = project.id;
      state.title = project.title || "Sem título";
      state.html = project.html || "";
      state.css = project.css || "";
      state.js = project.js || "";
      state.tab = "html";

      persistState();
      renderAll();
      updatePreview();
      closeOpenModal();
      showStatus("success", "Projeto aberto!");
    }
260. deleteProjectfunção · linhas 14849–14873 · usa 60, 63, 122, 131, 133, 150, 155, 157, 181, 183, 200, 205, 207, 233, 241, 258, 263, 292
    async function deleteProject(project) {
      if (!confirm('Excluir "' + project.title + '"?')) return;

      if (project.source === "cloud" && supabase) {
        const { error } = await supabase
          .from("snippets")
          .delete()
          .eq("id", project.id);

        if (error) {
          showStatus("error", "Erro: " + error.message);
          return;
        }

        await loadCloudProjects();
        showStatus("success", "Projeto excluído.");
        return;
      }

      state.localProjects = state.localProjects.filter((p) => p.id !== project.id);
      localStorage.setItem(LOCAL_PROJECTS_KEY, JSON.stringify(state.localProjects));
      renderProjects();
      showStatus("success", "Projeto local excluído.");
    }
261. renameProjectfunção · linhas 14874–14896 · usa 60, 131, 151, 155, 157, 181, 201, 205, 207, 233, 258, 263
    function renameProject(project) {
      const title = prompt("Novo nome:", project.title);
      if (!title || !title.trim()) return;

      if (project.source === "cloud" && supabase) {
        supabase
          .from("snippets")
          .update({ title: title.trim() })
          .eq("id", project.id)
          .then(() => loadCloudProjects());
        return;
      }

      const local = state.localProjects.find((p) => p.id === project.id);
      if (local) {
        local.title = title.trim();
        localStorage.setItem(LOCAL_PROJECTS_KEY, JSON.stringify(state.localProjects));
        renderProjects();
      }
    }

    // ===== RENDERIZAÇÃO =====
262. mergedProjectsfunção · linhas 14897–14902 · usa 131, 156, 181, 206, 233
    function mergedProjects() {
      const cloud = state.cloudProjects.map((p) => ({ ...p, source: "cloud" }));
      const local = state.localProjects.map((p) => ({ ...p, source: "local" }));
      return [...cloud, ...local];
    }
263. renderProjectsfunção · linhas 14903–14922 · usa 60, 118, 132, 156, 157, 158, 159, 182, 206, 207, 208, 209, 234, 262, 264, 265, 283
    function renderProjects() {
      const term = $("searchInput").value.trim().toLowerCase();
      const projects = mergedProjects().filter((p) =>
        String(p.title || "").toLowerCase().includes(term)
      );

      $("projectList").innerHTML = "";

      if (!projects.length) {
        $("projectList").innerHTML = '<div class="empty">Nenhum projeto.</div>';
        return;
      }

      projects.forEach((p, idx) => {
        $("projectList").appendChild(createProjectElement(p, idx + 1));
      });

      renderOpenProjects();
    }
264. createProjectElementfunção · linhas 14923–14965 · usa 63, 149, 150, 151, 158, 199, 200, 201, 208, 259, 260, 261
    function createProjectElement(project, number) {
      const item = document.createElement("div");
      item.className = "project-item";

      const info = document.createElement("div");
      info.className = "project-info";

      const name = document.createElement("div");
      name.className = "project-name";
      name.textContent = "#" + number + " — " + (project.title || "Sem título");

      const meta = document.createElement("div");
      meta.className = "project-meta";
      meta.textContent = (project.source === "cloud" ? "☁ Supabase" : "💾 Local");

      info.append(name, meta);
      info.addEventListener("click", () => loadProject(project));

      const actions = document.createElement("div");
      actions.className = "project-actions";

      const rename = document.createElement("button");
      rename.className = "small-btn";
      rename.textContent = "✎";
      rename.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        renameProject(project);
      });

      const del = document.createElement("button");
      del.className = "small-btn";
      del.textContent = "×";
      del.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        deleteProject(project);
      });

      actions.append(rename, del);
      item.append(info, actions);

      return item;
    }
265. renderOpenProjectsfunção · linhas 14966–14984 · usa 118, 132, 156, 158, 159, 182, 206, 208, 209, 234, 262, 264, 283
    function renderOpenProjects() {
      const term = $("openSearchInput").value.trim().toLowerCase();
      const container = $("openProjectList");
      container.innerHTML = "";

      const projects = mergedProjects().filter((p) =>
        String(p.title || "").toLowerCase().includes(term)
      );

      if (!projects.length) {
        container.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">Nenhum projeto.</div>';
        return;
      }

      projects.forEach((p, idx) => {
        container.appendChild(createProjectElement(p, idx + 1));
      });
    }
266. renderLayoutfunção · linhas 14985–15003 · usa 118, 131, 132, 160, 181, 182, 210, 233, 234, 283
    function renderLayout() {
      $("editorPanel").classList.toggle("collapsed", state.editorCollapsed);
      $("expandEditorBtn").style.display = state.editorCollapsed ? "inline-flex" : "none";
      $("collapseBtn").style.display = state.editorCollapsed ? "none" : "inline-flex";

      $("autoRunBtn").classList.toggle("primary", state.autoRun);
      $("autoRunBtn").textContent = state.autoRun ? "◉ Ao vivo" : "○ Manual";

      const isMobile = window.innerWidth <= 600;

      if (isMobile) {
        $("editorPanel").classList.toggle("mobile-hidden", state.mobileView !== "editor");
        $("previewPanel").classList.toggle("mobile-hidden", state.mobileView !== "preview");
      } else {
        $("editorPanel").classList.remove("mobile-hidden");
        $("previewPanel").classList.remove("mobile-hidden");
      }
    }

270. downloadFilefunção · linhas 15024–15037 · usa 164, 214
    function downloadFile(filename, content, type) {
      const blob = new Blob([content], { type });
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const link = document.createElement("a");

      link.href = url;
      link.download = filename;
      document.body.appendChild(link);
      link.click();
      link.remove();

      setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000);
    }

273. openFullscreenfunção · linhas 15056–15062 · usa 143, 193, 217, 251
    function openFullscreen() {

    }
274. importFilefunção · linhas 15063–15092 · usa 118, 122, 131, 132, 133, 137, 146, 161, 167, 181, 182, 183, 187, 196, 211, 218, 233, 234, 241, 245, 254, 267, 283, 292
    function importFile(file) {
      const reader = new FileReader();

      reader.onload = () => {
        const content = String(reader.result || "");
        const ext = file.name.split(".").pop().toLowerCase();

        state.title = file.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, "") || "Importado";

        if (ext === "css") {
          state.tab = "css";
          state.css = content;
        } else if (ext === "js") {
          state.tab = "js";
          state.js = content;
        } else {
          state.tab = "html";
          state.html = content;
        }

        state.currentId = null;
        persistState();
        renderAll();
        updatePreview();
        showStatus("success", "Arquivo importado.");
      };

      reader.readAsText(file);
    }
275. resetCurrentfunção · linhas 15093–15106 · usa 122, 128, 129, 130, 131, 133, 137, 146, 161, 168, 178, 179, 180, 181, 183, 187, 196, 211, 219, 229, 230, 231, 233, 241, 245, 254, 267, 292

      state.tab = "html";

    }

    // ===== EVENTOS =====
279. bindEventsfunção · linhas 15134–15233 · usa 60, 118, 122, 131, 132, 133, 137, 139, 141, 146, 148, 154, 155, 157, 159, 160, 162, 163, 165, 166, 167, 168, 169, 172, 174, 181, 182, 183, 187, 189, 191, 196, 198, 204, 205, 207, 209, 210, 212, 213, 215, 216, 217, 218, 219, 220, 223, 225, 233, 234, 236, 237, 240, 241, 245, 247, 249, 254, 256, 257, 258, 263, 265, 266, 268, 269, 271, 272, 273, 274, 275, 276, 277, 283, 292
    function bindEvents() {
      // Login
      $("loginBtn").addEventListener("click", handleLogin);
      $("signupBtn").addEventListener("click", handleSignup);

      // Abas
      document.querySelectorAll("[data-tab]").forEach((btn) => {
        btn.addEventListener("click", () => setTab(btn.dataset.tab));
      });

      // Título
      $("projectTitle").addEventListener("input", (e) => {
        state.title = e.target.value;
        persistState();
      });

      // Editor
      $("codeEditor").addEventListener("input", (e) => {
        setCurrentCode(e.target.value);
      });

      // Botões
      $("autoRunBtn").addEventListener("click", () => {
        state.autoRun = !state.autoRun;
        persistState();
        renderLayout();
        if (state.autoRun) updatePreview();
      });

      $("runBtn").addEventListener("click", updatePreview);
      $("saveLocalBtn").addEventListener("click", saveLocal);
      $("saveCloudBtn").addEventListener("click", saveCloud);
      $("openBtn").addEventListener("click", openOpenModal);
      $("closeOpenBtn").addEventListener("click", closeOpenModal);
      $("copyBtn").addEventListener("click", copyCurrent);
      $("downloadBtn").addEventListener("click", downloadCurrent);
      $("fullscreenBtn").addEventListener("click", openFullscreen);
      $("resetBtn").addEventListener("click", resetCurrent);
      $("clearBtn").addEventListener("click", clearCurrent);
      $("themeBtn").addEventListener("click", toggleTheme);
      $("logoutBtn").addEventListener("click", handleLogout);

      // Collapse
      $("collapseBtn").addEventListener("click", () => {
        state.editorCollapsed = true;
        persistState();
        renderLayout();
      });

      $("expandEditorBtn").addEventListener("click", () => {
        state.editorCollapsed = false;
        persistState();
        renderLayout();
      });

      // Mobile
      $("mobileEditorBtn").addEventListener("click", () => {
        state.mobileView = "editor";
        renderLayout();
      });

      $("mobilePreviewBtn").addEventListener("click", () => {
        state.mobileView = "preview";
        updatePreview();
        renderLayout();
      });

      // Config
      $("configBtn").addEventListener("click", () => {
        $("configModal").classList.add("open");
      });

      $("closeConfigBtn").addEventListener("click", () => {
        $("configModal").classList.remove("open");
      });

      // Pesquisa
      $("searchInput").addEventListener("input", renderProjects);
      $("openSearchInput").addEventListener("input", renderOpenProjects);

      // Atualizar
      $("refreshBtn").addEventListener("click", () => {
        loadCloudProjects();
        showStatus("info", "Atualizado!");
      });

      // Import
      $("importBtn").addEventListener("click", () => $("fileInput").click());
      $("fileInput").addEventListener("change", (e) => {
        const file = e.target.files[0];
        if (file) importFile(file);
        e.target.value = "";
      });

      // Resize
      window.addEventListener("resize", renderLayout);
    }

284. initSupabasefunção · linhas 15662–15680 · usa 119, 235, 285, 286
    async function initSupabase() {
      const url = localStorage.getItem("supabase_url");
      const key = localStorage.getItem("supabase_key");

      if (!url || !key) return false;

      supabase = window.supabase.createClient(url, key);

      const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
      currentUser = user;

      if (!currentUser) return false;

      await loadUserConfig();
      await loadSessions();

      return true;
    }
285. loadUserConfigfunção · linhas 15681–15692
    async function loadUserConfig() {
      if (!supabase || !currentUser) return;

      const { data } = await supabase
        .from("user_configs")
        .select("*")
        .eq("user_id", currentUser.id)
        .single();

      userConfig = data;
    }
286. loadSessionsfunção · linhas 15693–15717 · usa 118, 132, 182, 234, 283, 287
    async function loadSessions() {
      if (!supabase || !currentUser) return;

      const { data } = await supabase
        .from("chat_sessions")
        .select("*")
        .eq("user_id", currentUser.id)
        .order("created_at", { ascending: false });

      $("sessionsList").innerHTML = "";

      if (!data || data.length === 0) {
        $("sessionsList").innerHTML = '<div style="color: var(--muted); font-size: 11px;">Nenhuma conversa</div>';
        return;
      }

      data.forEach(session => {
        const item = document.createElement("div");
        item.className = "session-item";
        item.textContent = session.title;
        item.addEventListener("click", () => loadSession(session));
        $("sessionsList").appendChild(item);
      });
    }
287. loadSessionfunção · linhas 15718–15749 · usa 118, 132, 182, 234, 283, 290, 291
    async function loadSession(session) {
      currentSession = session;
      brainSystem = new BrainSystem(supabase, session.id);

      await brainSystem.loadMemories();
      await brainSystem.loadCodeFragments();

      document.querySelectorAll(".session-item").forEach(item => {
        item.classList.remove("active");
      });

      event.target.classList.add("active");

      $("sessionTitle").textContent = session.title;

      const { data: messages } = await supabase
        .from("chat_messages")
        .select("*")
        .eq("session_id", session.id)
        .order("created_at", { ascending: true });

      $("chatMessages").innerHTML = "";

      if (messages && messages.length > 0) {
        messages.forEach(msg => {
          addMessageToUI(msg.role, msg.content);
        });
      }

      updateTokenDisplay();
    }
288. createNewSessionfunção · linhas 15750–15778 · usa 122, 133, 183, 241, 286, 287, 292
    async function createNewSession() {
      if (!supabase || !currentUser) return;

      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from("chat_sessions")
        .insert({
          user_id: currentUser.id,
          title: "Conversa " + new Date().toLocaleTimeString(),
          model: tokenManager.currentModel,
          temperature: 0.7,
          max_tokens: tokenManager.getModelInfo().maxTokensPerRequest
        })
        .select()
        .single();

      if (error) {
        showStatus("Erro ao criar conversa", "error");
        return;
      }

      currentSession = data;
      brainSystem = new BrainSystem(supabase, data.id);
      tokenManager.reset();

      await loadSessions();
      await loadSession(data);
      showStatus("Nova conversa criada");
    }
289. sendMessagefunção · linhas 15779–15885 · usa 118, 122, 132, 133, 182, 183, 234, 241, 283, 290, 291, 292
    async function sendMessage() {
      const text = $("chatInput").value.trim();

      if (!text) return;

      if (!currentSession) {
        showStatus("Selecione uma conversa primeiro", "error");
        return;
      }

      if (!userConfig?.openai_key) {
        showStatus("Configure sua chave OpenAI", "error");
        return;
      }

      // Verificar tokens
      const estimatedTokens = tokenManager.estimateTokens(text);
      const canRequest = tokenManager.canMakeRequest(estimatedTokens);

      if (!canRequest.allowed) {
        showStatus("❌ " + canRequest.reason, "error");
        return;
      }

      // Construir prompt com contexto do cérebro
      let finalPrompt = brainSystem.buildContinuationPrompt(text, tokenManager);
      const model = tokenManager.getModelInfo();
      finalPrompt += brainSystem.buildFragmentationInstructions(model.maxCodeLines);

      // Adicionar mensagem do usuário
      addMessageToUI("user", text);

      await supabase.from("chat_messages").insert({
        session_id: currentSession.id,
        role: "user",
        content: text,
        tokens_used: estimatedTokens
      });

      $("chatInput").value = "";

      // Chamar IA
      try {
        showStatus("⏳ Aguardando resposta...", "");

        const response = await fetch("https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: {
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            "Authorization": `Bearer ${userConfig.openai_key}`
          },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            model: currentSession.model,
            messages: [{ role: "user", content: finalPrompt }],
            max_tokens: currentSession.max_tokens,
            temperature: currentSession.temperature
          })
        });

        if (!response.ok) {
          const error = await response.json();
          throw new Error(error.error?.message || "Erro na API");
        }

        const data = await response.json();
        const aiMessage = data.choices[0].message.content;
        const inputTokens = data.usage.prompt_tokens;
        const outputTokens = data.usage.completion_tokens;

        addMessageToUI("assistant", aiMessage);

        // Extrair código
        const codeBlocks = brainSystem.extractCodeBlocks(aiMessage);

        // Salvar mensagem
        await supabase.from("chat_messages").insert({
          session_id: currentSession.id,
          role: "assistant",
          content: aiMessage,
          tokens_used: outputTokens
        });

        // Salvar no cérebro
        await brainSystem.saveMemory(text, aiMessage, codeBlocks);

        // Registrar uso
        const tokenData = tokenManager.addTokens(inputTokens, outputTokens);

        await supabase.from("ai_usage").insert({
          user_id: currentUser.id,
          session_id: currentSession.id,
          model: currentSession.model,
          tokens_input: inputTokens,
          tokens_output: outputTokens,
          tokens_total: tokenData.totalTokens,
          cost: tokenData.cost
        });

        updateTokenDisplay();
        showStatus("✅ Resposta recebida", "success");

      } catch (error) {
        showStatus("❌ Erro: " + error.message, "error");
        addMessageToUI("system", "Erro ao conectar com IA: " + error.message);
      }
    }
290. addMessageToUIfunção · linhas 15886–15908 · usa 118, 132, 182, 234, 283
    function addMessageToUI(role, content) {
      const container = $("chatMessages");

      if (container.querySelector(".empty")) {
        container.innerHTML = "";
      }

      const msg = document.createElement("div");
      msg.className = "message " + role;

      // Formatar código
      if (role === "assistant") {
        msg.innerHTML = content
          .replace(/```(\w+)?\n([\s\S]*?)```/g, '<pre><code>$2</code></pre>')
          .replace(/`([^`]+)`/g, '<code>$1</code>');
      } else {
        msg.textContent = content;
      }

      container.appendChild(msg);
      container.scrollTop = container.scrollHeight;
    }
291. updateTokenDisplayfunção · linhas 15909–15929 · usa 118, 132, 182, 234, 283
    function updateTokenDisplay() {
      const progress = tokenManager.getProgress();

      $("tokenFill").style.width = progress.dailyPercent + "%";
      $("tokenFill").className = "token-fill";

      if (progress.dailyPercent > 80) {
        $("tokenFill").classList.add("danger");
      } else if (progress.dailyPercent > 50) {
        $("tokenFill").classList.add("warning");
      }

      $("tokenText").textContent = `${progress.dailyUsed} / ${progress.dailyMax}`;
      $("modelInfo").textContent = progress.model;
      $("sessionTokens").textContent = progress.sessionUsed;

      // Calcular custo
      const cost = tokenManager.calculateCost(progress.sessionUsed);
      $("costInfo").textContent = "R$ " + (cost * 5.5).toFixed(2); // Conversão USD para BRL
    }
292. showStatusfunção · linhas 15930–15941 · usa 118, 122, 132, 133, 182, 183, 234, 241, 283
    function showStatus(message, type = "success") {
      const el = $("status");
      el.className = "status show " + type;
      el.textContent = message;

      if (type === "success") {
        setTimeout(() => {
          el.className = "status";
        }, 3000);
      }
    }

298. $(…)chamada · linhas 15968–16008 · usa 118, 122, 132, 133, 182, 183, 234, 241, 283, 292
    $("exportBtn").addEventListener("click", async () => {
      if (!currentSession) return;

      const { data: messages } = await supabase
        .from("chat_messages")
        .select("*")
        .eq("session_id", currentSession.id)
        .order("created_at", { ascending: true });

      const { data: brain } = await supabase
        .from("brain_history")
        .select("*")
        .eq("session_id", currentSession.id)
        .order("iteration", { ascending: true });

      const { data: code } = await supabase
        .from("code_fragments")
        .select("*")
        .eq("session_id", currentSession.id)
        .order("fragment_order", { ascending: true });

      const exportData = {
        session: currentSession,
        messages,
        brain,
        code,
        exportedAt: new Date().toISOString()
      };

      const json = JSON.stringify(exportData, null, 2);
      const blob = new Blob([json], { type: "application/json" });
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const link = document.createElement("a");
      link.href = url;
      link.download = currentSession.title + "-completo.json";
      link.click();
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

      showStatus("Exportado com sucesso!");
    });
